```diff

+++ microsoft_vscode-docs::release-notes/v1_146.md (new: 8c558cdf)
@@ -73,11 +73,13 @@
 
 To open a chat in a new window, right-click the session or chat in the sessions list and select **Open in** > **New Window**. You can also drag a chat out of the Agents window to create a new window. You can move it back with the **Move to Main Window** command.
 
+The following video shows opening a chat in its own window.
+
+<video src="images/1_146/chat-windows-open.mp4" title="Video showing a chat opened in a new window from the Agents window, with the chat beside its editors." autoplay loop controls muted></video>
+
 A chat window can hold several chats from one or more sessions: pick another session in its title bar, drag a chat in from another window, or select **New Session** (``Shortcut: sessions.chatWindow.newSession``) at the left of the title bar to start a new session in that window. Chat windows reopen with their chats and editors after you restart VS Code.
 
 The content within a chat window is organized into parts, which you can arrange freely to fit your workflow.
-
-<video src="images/1_146/chat-windows.mp4" title="Video showing moving a chat into a new window and arranging parts in a chat window." autoplay loop controls muted></video>
 
 * **Move parts:** Drag a part by the grabber on the top edge of its card to another side of the window, or beside another part. You can also use **Move Part Left**, **Move Part Right**, **Move Part Up**, and **Move Part Down**.
 * **Move tabs between parts:** Drag a tab to another part, or drop it on a part's edge to split that part. Use **Move Editor to Part** or **Move Editor into New Part Left/Right/Above/Below** to do the same from the Command Palette. Drag an editor tab onto the chat input to attach it to the chat as context.
@@ -85,6 +87,10 @@
 * **Switch the layout:** Use **Configure Layout** in the title bar to show the chat, the editor, or both, side by side or stacked. In the stacked layout, **Agents: Toggle Chat Conversation** collapses the chat to only its input, so the editor takes the rest of the window.
 
 Use **Developer: Reset Chat Window Layout** to return a window to how it first opened.
+
+The following video shows rearranging a chat window and resetting its layout.
+
+<video src="images/1_146/chat-windows-layout.mp4" title="Video showing parts and tabs of a chat window being rearranged, and the layout being reset." autoplay loop controls muted></video>
 
 Let us know what you think by [filing an issue](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/issues).
 
@@ -217,6 +223,10 @@
 ### Mermaid 12 support
 
 VS Code now uses Mermaid 12 when rendering Mermaid diagrams in chat, Markdown previews, and notebooks. You can use new diagram types, including use-case diagrams, and benefit from updated automatic layout behavior.
+
+| Before: Mermaid 11 | After: Mermaid 12 |
+| --- | --- |
+| ![Screenshot showing Mermaid 11 rendering with older flowchart and subgraph layouts and a use-case diagram error.](images/1_146/mermaid-11.png) | ![Screenshot showing Mermaid 12 rendering with updated flowchart and subgraph layouts and a rendered use-case diagram.](images/1_146/mermaid-12.png) |
 
 ### Copy the address of local HTML files in the Integrated Browser
 
```

Name matching is exact and case-insensitive; falls back to the auto-generated summary when no explicit name matches. Conflicts with `--continue`.  | `--session-id ID`                  | Use an exact session or task ID when you do not want `--resume`'s broader matching by ID prefix or session name. Names and ID prefixes do not create new sessions. The trust is not saved to `trustedFolders` and is not a tool or path permission grant. Cannot be combined with `--resume`, `--continue`, or `--session-id`. |

 | `COPILOT_ALLOW_ALL` | Allow all permissions automatically (equivalent to `--allow-all`). @@ -1641,6 +1641,17 @@
 When a shell command needs tool approval and access to paths outside the allowed directories, the CLI can combine both approvals in one prompt. Approving once grants path access for that run only. Approving for the session or location also grants path access for the rest of the session.
 
 Use `/reset-allowed-tools` to clear session approvals for the current session.
+
+### Parent-domain URL approvals
+
+URL approval prompts can offer a parent-domain scope, such as `https://*.example.com`, in addition to the exact origin. This approves every subdomain of the registrable domain, such as `api.example.com` and `flow.example.com`, for the rest of the session.
+
+The scope has these restrictions:
+
+* Approval applies only to the current session and is never saved to settings.
+* The scheme and any non-default port must match.
+* Hosts under a separately owned suffix, such as `s3.amazonaws.com`, are not covered by approving `*.amazonaws.com`. This keeps an approval for *.amazonaws.com from extending to buckets under s3.amazonaws.com, which may belong to other tenants.
+* IP addresses, single-label hosts such as `localhost`, and public suffixes such as `github.io` do not get this option.
 
 ## Security

 | `--fleet`                     | Run the prompt in fleet mode, so Copilot uses parallel subagents to work on separate parts of the task. Combine with `-p` for non-interactive automation, `-i` for an interactive session, or a piped prompt. Not supported in ACP server mode. See [Speed Up Task Completion](https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/how-tos/copilot-cli/use-copilot-cli/speed-up-task-completion). |
 | `--model=MODEL`               | Choose the AI model (for example, `gpt-6-astra` or `claude-haiku-4.5`). Useful for pinning a model in reproducible workflows. See [Choosing a model](#choosing-a-model) below. |
 | `--no-ask-user`               | Prevent the agent from pausing to seek additional user input. |
+| `--no-auto-update`            | Disable automatic CLI updates and prevent a newer cached version from running instead of the installed version. |
 | `--output-format=FORMAT`      | Set the output format: `text` (the default) or `json`. With `json`, the CLI emits JSONL (one JSON object per line), which is convenient for parsing the agent's output in scripts. |
 | `--secret-env-vars=VAR ...`   | An environment variable whose value you want redacted in output. For multiple variables, use a quoted, comma-separated list. Essential for preventing secrets being exposed in logs. The values in the `GITHUB_TOKEN` and `COPILOT_GITHUB_TOKEN` environment variables are redacted by default. |
 | `--trust-folder`              | Trust the working directory's hooks, workspace MCP servers, and repository plugin settings for a single new non-interactive session (`-p` or piped standard input). The trust is not saved to `trustedFolders`, does not extend to parent or sibling directories, and does not grant tool, path, or URL permissions. Managed policy still applies. Cannot be combined with `--resume`, `--continue`, or `--session-id`. Only use this for configuration you have reviewed. |

 | `COPILOT_ASSISTED_APPROVAL` | Set to `true` to turn on [copilot.assisted_permissions] for a non-interactive run |
 | `COPILOT_MODEL`       | Set the model (for example, `gpt-6-astra`, `claude-haiku-4.5`) |
 | `COPILOT_HOME`        | Set the directory for the CLI configuration file (`~/.copilot` by default) |
-| `COPILOT_AUTO_UPDATE` | Set to `false` to disable automatic updates. Useful in CI and other automated environments where you want to pin the CLI version. |
+| `COPILOT_AUTO_UPDATE` | Set to `false` to disable automatic updates and prevent a newer cached CLI version from running instead of the installed version. Useful in CI and other automated environments where you want to pin the CLI version. |

 For full details of environment variables for Copilot CLI, use the command `copilot help environment` in your terminal.
+
+## Pinning the CLI version
+
+To downgrade or install a specific version, use `npm install -g @github/copilot@VERSION`, replacing `VERSION` with the release version, or download the release directly. See [`github/copilot-cli` releases](https://github.com/github/copilot-cli/releases).
+
+Start the CLI with `--no-auto-update` or `COPILOT_AUTO_UPDATE=false` to keep using that version. Otherwise, a newer version in the package cache can run instead, and automatic updates can download the latest version again.
 
 ## Using [copilot.assisted_permissions]

+| `showTimestamps` | `boolean` | `true` | Show dim `HH:mm` timestamps next to your messages in the timeline. Assistant replies and tool calls do not get timestamps. |
 | `showTipsOnStartup` | `boolean` | `true` | Show a random command tip when the CLI starts. |
 | `sidebar` | `boolean` | `true` | Enable the current-session sidebar. Set to `false` to disable it entirely, hiding the composer hint and the <kbd>←</kbd> open gesture. See [Cli Command Reference](https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/copilot-cli-reference/cli-command-reference#sidebar-and-sessions-tab-shortcuts). |
 | `skillDirectories` | `string[]` | `[]` | Additional directories to search for custom skill definitions (in addition to `~/.copilot/skills/`). |
@@ -833,12 +833,12 @@
 | `disableAllHooks` | `boolean` | Repository takes precedence | Disable all hooks. |
 | `disabledMcpServers` | `string[]` | Union—repository can add entries, never remove | MCP servers configured but not started. |
 | `disabledSkills` | `string[]` | Union—repository can add entries, never remove | Skills discovered but not loaded. |
-| `effortLevel` | `string` | Replaced—repository takes precedence | Pin the default reasoning effort. |
+| `effortLevel` | `string` | Replaced—repository takes precedence | Pin the default reasoning effort. Accepts `none`, `minimal`, `low`, `medium`, `high`, `xhigh`, or `max`, depending on the model. When unset, the model's default applies, which is `medium` for most models. |
 | `enabledPlugins` | `Record<string, boolean>` | Merged—repository overrides user for same key | Declarative plugin auto-install. |
 | `extraKnownMarketplaces` | `Record<string, {...}>` | Merged—repository overrides user for same key | Plugin marketplaces available in this repository. Each entry's `source` is required. An `autoUpdate: true` on an entry is accepted at the repository level but currently ignored—the auto-update opt-in is only honored when set in the user's own settings or in managed (MDM/server) settings. |
 | `hooks` | `object` | Merged—repository overrides user for same key | Hook definitions scoped to this repository. See [Use Hooks](https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/how-tos/copilot-cli/customize-copilot/use-hooks). |
 | `includeCoAuthoredBy` | `boolean` | Replaced—repository takes precedence | Add a `Co-authored-by` trailer to commits. |
-| `mergeStrategy` | `"rebase"` \| `"merge"` | Replaced—repository takes precedence | Conflict resolution strategy for `/pr fix conflicts`. |
+| `mergeStrategy` | `"rebase"` \| `"merge"` | Replaced—repository takes precedence | How `/pr fix conflicts`, `/pr fix all`, `/pr auto`, and `/pr agentmerge` bring the base branch into your pull request branch. `rebase` force-pushes with `--force-with-lease`, while `merge` pushes a merge commit. When unset, `/pr fix` commands ask each time and `/pr auto` lets the agent choose. |
 | `model` | `string` | Replaced—repository takes precedence | Pin the default model for this repo. |
 | `respectGitignore` | `boolean` | Tighten-only—repository can enable, never disable | Exclude gitignored files from the `@` file mention picker. |
 
@@ -939,6 +939,7 @@
 | `remoteControl` | Control whether sessions on this device can be controlled from other devices. `mode` is `"enabled"`, `"disabled"`, or `"requireSSO"` (requires `githubDotComOrganizations` when set). |
 | `requireBase64ImageAttachments` | When `true`, require base64-encoded image attachments instead of attachment URLs. `false` or unset keeps existing behavior. |
 | `sandbox` | Set a sandbox policy floor that users cannot relax. Supported settings include `enabled`, `failIfUnavailable`, `allowBypass`, `addCurrentWorkingDirectory`, `sandboxMcpServers`, `sandboxLspServers`, `auth.git`, `auth.gh`, `allowDevToolAccess`, `learningMode`, and the `userPolicy.*` filesystem and network rules. The managed value always takes precedence over a user's own value in the safer direction. Turning the sandbox on, requiring it to succeed, and sandboxing MCP and LSP servers cannot be turned off. Disabling bypass or credential injection cannot be re-enabled. Filesystem allow lists can only be narrowed, and denied paths can only be added to. `failIfUnavailable` can only be set by an administrator and blocks the session when the sandbox cannot be established. For the settings users can set themselves, see [User settings](#user-settings-copilotsettingsjson) or run `copilot help sandbox`. |
+| `sandbox.sandboxLspServers` | Boolean, defaults to `true`. When sandboxing is enabled, language servers start under the session's sandbox policy. Set to `false` to run language servers outside the sandbox, subject to the managed sandbox policy floor. |
 | `shellShortcut` | Force-enable or force-disable the `$` interactive shell shortcut for all users. A managed value always overrides the user's own `shellShortcut` setting. |
 | `strictKnownMarketplaces` | Restrict plugins to an allowlist of known marketplaces (a JSON array of marketplace specs). The allowlist also governs built-in marketplaces once set—an empty array (`[]`) hides and blocks every marketplace, including built-ins, not just user- or repository-added ones. |
 | `telemetry` | Push baseline OpenTelemetry export configuration: `enabled`, `endpoint`, `protocol`, `headers`, `resourceAttributes`, `captureContent`, `lockCaptureContent`, `serviceName`, and `headersProvider`. See [Opentelemetry Reference](https://docs.github.com/en/copilot/reference/enterprise-administrators/opentelemetry-reference). |
```

v1.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">In <a href="/oss/python/langchain/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a>, tool-call arguments now stream as <code>tool-call-delta</code> events. Each event carries only the new part of the arguments, as <code>text-delta</code> does for text. Earlier versions sent <code>block-delta</code> events that repeated all of the arguments so far.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="upgrade-clients-before-the-server" aria-labelledby="_R_s35crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#upgrade-clients-before-the-server" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Upgrade clients before the server">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_s35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Upgrade clients before the server</span></h3><span data-as="p">Clients older than the versions below do not handle <code>tool-call-delta</code>. For example, earlier versions of <code>@langchain/react</code> can stop updating a <code>useMessages</code> view. The <a href="/langsmith/agent-server" class="link">Agent Server</a> passes events through unchanged, so the <code>langchain-core</code> version on the server decides which events clients receive.</span><ol>

justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_c35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langchain</code> v1.5.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">LangChain 1.5 adds decision models and decision-driven middleware. This entry also highlights recent improvements across core and integration packages since LangChain 1.4.0.</span><ul>
+<li><strong>Python 3.11 or later required</strong>: <code>langchain</code> 1.5 and <code>langchain-core</code> 1.8 drop Python 3.10 support. Upgrade Python before upgrading these packages.</li>
+<li><strong>Decisions API</strong>: <code>langchain_core.decisions</code> introduces <code>BaseDecisionModel</code> for typed, probabilistic answers about text, messages, and structured state. Questions support binary judgments (<code>Noul</code>), categorical choices (<code>Choice</code>), ordinal scores (<code>Score</code>), and bounding boxes (<code>Box</code>). (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41765" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41765</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>Decision-driven middleware (beta)</strong>: <code>ModelRouterMiddleware</code> selects a model for each run from your configured choices and instructions. <code>AutoModeMiddleware</code> classifies configured tool calls and blocks those it judges risky. Versions of these middleware were previously released in <code>langchain-typesafe</code>. They now require an explicit decision model through <code>classifier=</code>. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41766" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41766</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>TypeSafe 0.1</strong>: <code>TypeSafeClassifier</code> implements the Decisions API and adds image inputs and <code>Box</code> answers. Its experimental middleware imports are deprecated: use <code>langchain.agents.middleware</code> with <code>classifier=TypeSafeClassifier()</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/integrations/providers/typesafe" class="link">TypeSafe integration guide</a> for usage and migration details.</li>
+<li><strong>Consistent refusal handling</strong>: OpenAI and Anthropic refusals use standard <code>refusal</code> content blocks. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/models#handle-a-model-refusal" class="link">Handle a model refusal</a>.</li>
+<li><strong>Summarization improvements</strong>: <code>SummarizationMiddleware</code> accepts a separate model or custom callable through <code>summarizer=</code>.</li>
+<li><strong>Mid-conversation updates</strong>: Supported models accept <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/anthropic#mid-conversation-system-messages" class="link">Anthropic system and tool updates</a>, <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#add-tools-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI Responses tool additions</a>, and <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#changing-reasoning-effort-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI reasoning-effort changes</a> without restarting the conversation.</li>
+</ul></div></div></div>

 <li><strong>Typed answers</strong>: each interrupt carries a <code>response_schema</code> listing the decisions that call allows, so a client can build its form from the schema. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#read-the-response-schema" class="link">Read the response schema</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Checked before saving</strong>: LangGraph rejects an invalid answer before it is saved, so you can correct it and answer the same interrupt again. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#correct-an-invalid-answer" class="link">Correct an invalid answer</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Edits stay on the called tool</strong>: an <code>edit</code> can’t switch tools, and its arguments are checked against the tool’s own schema.</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Use per-call mode for new agents. LangChain plans to make it the default in the next major release. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#pause-once-per-tool-call" class="link">Pause once per tool call</a> and the <a href="/oss/python/concepts/human-in-the-loop" class="link">human-in-the-loop overview</a>.</span></div></div></div>

[&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">The <code>langchain.mcp</code> namespace is in beta. Importing from it raises a <code>LangChainBetaWarning</code>.</span></div></div><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>One adapter, any transport</strong>: <code>MCPAdapter</code> infers the transport from its target — a URL, a local script over stdio, an in-process server, an <code>MCPConfig</code> dict for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/connections#multiple-servers" class="link">multiple servers</a>, or a pre-built <code>fastmcp.Client</code>. <code>await adapter.list_tools()</code> returns LangChain tools ready for <code>create_agent</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp" class="link">MCP overview</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Interrupt-driven elicitation</strong>: when a server asks for input mid-call, <code>MCPAdapter</code> surfaces the question as a LangGraph <code>interrupt()</code>, so a human answers and the run resumes. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#elicitation" class="link">Elicitation</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Authentication through FastMCP</strong>: bearer tokens, full OAuth 2.1 with dynamic client registration, or any <code>httpx.Auth</code>, including <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth#per-user-authentication" class="link">per-user authentication</a> in a deployment. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth" class="link">Authentication</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Richer tool metadata</strong>: each tool carries its MCP provenance under an <code>mcp</code> namespace on its metadata, including annotations such as <code>destructive_hint</code> for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#human-in-the-loop" class="link">gating destructive tools behind approval</a>. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#tool-metadata" class="link">Tool metadata</a>.</li>
-</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="migrating" aria-labelledby="_R_1s75crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#migrating" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Migrating">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s75crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Migrating</span></h3><span data-as="p">Moving from <code>langchain-mcp-adapters</code>? <code>MultiServerMCPClient</code> is replaced by <code>MCPAdapter</code>, and several features have changed or been removed. See the <a href="/oss/python/migrate/langchain-mcp-adapters" class="link">migration guide</a>.</span></div></div></div>

On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our <a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">revamped evaluation suite</a> with no quality regression.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="optimizations" aria-labelledby="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#optimizations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Optimizations">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Optimizations</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Lean prompts by default</strong>: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop <strong>43%</strong> (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4979</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#5009</a>)</li>
-</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
+</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware" class="link">Override a default middleware instance</a></strong>: A <code>middleware=</code> (or subagent <code>middleware</code>) instance whose <code>.name</code> matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own <code>SummarizationMiddleware(...)</code> to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4251</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Filesystem tools</strong>: New <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools" class="link"><code>delete</code></a> tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#3659</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#3851</a>); <code>write_file</code> now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4109</a>); <code>FilesystemMiddleware</code> accepts a <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access" class="link">tool allowlist</a> to expose only selected built-in tools (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4325</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4698</a>); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated <code>read_file</code> reports total and remaining lines plus the next <code>offset</code> (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4540</a>), <code>grep</code>/<code>glob</code> return partial results with a <code>truncated</code> flag instead of hanging on large trees (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4063</a>), and <code>grep</code> gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4570</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4706</a>).</li>
 <li><strong>More prompt-caching support</strong>: Bedrock prompt caching via the <code>deepagents[aws]</code> extra (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4108</a>), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4598</a>).</li>
 <li><strong>NVIDIA support</strong>: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4192</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4455</a>)</li>
-</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><ul>
+</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1sb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1sb5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Planning todos are opt-in</strong>: <code>create_deep_agent</code> no longer includes <code>TodoListMiddleware</code> by default, so the <code>write_todos</code> tool, <code>todos</code> state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with <code>middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]</code>. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4929</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Backend compatibility shims removed</strong>: Pass concrete <code>BackendProtocol</code> instances instead of factories, configure <code>StoreBackend</code> with an explicit <code>namespace</code>, and use the current <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> / <code>grep</code> / <code>ReadResult</code> APIs. Removed symbols include <code>BackendFactory</code>, <code>BACKEND_TYPES</code>, <code>FileFormat</code>, and <code>Unset</code>. New files store string <code>FileData.content</code>; older <code>list[str]</code> content stays readable and converts on next write. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4541</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Output format changes</strong>: Empty <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> output is now <code>No files found</code> instead of <code>[]</code>, and <code>read_file</code> no longer renders a fixed-width <code>cat -n</code>-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4561</a>)</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:</span><div class="prompt my-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 border-stone-100 bg-stone-50 dark:border-stone-800 dark:bg-stone-900 text-sm/[20px] text-stone-900 tracking-[-0.1px] dark:text-stone-50 print:print-color-exact"><div class="not-prose flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2" data-component-part="prompt-content-wrapper"><span aria-hidden="true" class="flex h-[1lh] shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center" data-component-part="prompt-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! bg-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-100 m-0! size-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/tabler/v3.44.0/outline/arrow-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/tabler/v3.44.0/outline/arrow-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;mask-size:100%"></svg></span><div class="min-w-0" data-component-part="prompt-description"><div class="space-y-4 whitespace-normal"><p>Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.</p></div></div></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden" data-component-part="prompt-actions-wrapper"><button class="px-2 py-1 flex items-center gap-1 justify-center rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-70 outline-offset-4 text-xs/[16px] whitespace-nowrap font-medium border-transparent bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary-dark" data-component-part="prompt-action-copy-button" type="button" aria-label="Copy prompt"><span aria-hidden="true" class="grid [&amp;&gt;*]:col-start-1 [&amp;&gt;*]:row-start-1 [&amp;&gt;*]:flex [&amp;&gt;*]:items-center [&amp;&gt;*]:justify-center [&amp;&gt;*]:gap-1"><span class="invisible"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-2.5 shrink-0 text-white dark:text-white"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Check"></use></svg>Copied</span><span class="">Copy prompt</span></span><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></button></div></div></div></div></div>

stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_cd5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.6.0</span></h2><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters" class="link"><code>CodeInterpreterMiddleware</code></a></strong>: (experimental) <code>deepagents</code> now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.</li>
 <li>Supports <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> in <code>stream_events</code> / <code>astream_events</code>. Refer to the <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a> guide for details.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): Deep Agents now uses <code>DeltaChannel</code> for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.</li>
@@ -1160,8 +1169,8 @@
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/profiles" class="link">Harness profiles</a></strong>: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (<code>HarnessProfile</code>) that <code>create_deep_agent</code> applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend" class="link"><code>ContextHubBackend</code></a></strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>

data-as="p">This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.</span><ul>
 <li>
 <span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong>: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use <code>snapshot_frequency=K</code> to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.</span>
 </li>
@@ -1178,7 +1187,7 @@
 <span data-as="p"><strong>New event streaming API (beta)</strong>: Pass <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> to <code>stream_events()</code> / <code>astream_events()</code> for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (<code>run.values</code>, <code>run.messages</code>, <code>run.lifecycle</code>, <code>run.subgraphs</code>) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. <code>run.messages</code> yields one <code>ChatModelStream</code> per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. <code>version=&quot;v1&quot;</code> and <code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code> are unchanged.</span>
 </li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Timeouts and error handlers are Python-only; retry policies continue to work in both Python and TypeScript.</span></div></div></div>

v1.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">In <a href="/oss/python/langchain/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a>, tool-call arguments now stream as <code>tool-call-delta</code> events. Each event carries only the new part of the arguments, as <code>text-delta</code> does for text. Earlier versions sent <code>block-delta</code> events that repeated all of the arguments so far.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="upgrade-clients-before-the-server" aria-labelledby="_R_s35crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#upgrade-clients-before-the-server" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Upgrade clients before the server">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_s35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Upgrade clients before the server</span></h3><span data-as="p">Clients older than the versions below do not handle <code>tool-call-delta</code>. For example, earlier versions of <code>@langchain/react</code> can stop updating a <code>useMessages</code> view. The <a href="/langsmith/agent-server" class="link">Agent Server</a> passes events through unchanged, so the <code>langchain-core</code> version on the server decides which events clients receive.</span><ol>

hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_c35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>langchain</code> v1.5.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">LangChain 1.5 adds decision models and decision-driven middleware. This entry also highlights recent improvements across core and integration packages since LangChain 1.4.0.</span><ul>
+<li><strong>Python 3.11 or later required</strong>: <code>langchain</code> 1.5 and <code>langchain-core</code> 1.8 drop Python 3.10 support. Upgrade Python before upgrading these packages.</li>
+<li><strong>Decisions API</strong>: <code>langchain_core.decisions</code> introduces <code>BaseDecisionModel</code> for typed, probabilistic answers about text, messages, and structured state. Questions support binary judgments (<code>Noul</code>), categorical choices (<code>Choice</code>), ordinal scores (<code>Score</code>), and bounding boxes (<code>Box</code>). (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41765" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41765</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>Decision-driven middleware (beta)</strong>: <code>ModelRouterMiddleware</code> selects a model for each run from your configured choices and instructions. <code>AutoModeMiddleware</code> classifies configured tool calls and blocks those it judges risky. Versions of these middleware were previously released in <code>langchain-typesafe</code>. They now require an explicit decision model through <code>classifier=</code>. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/langchain/pull/41766" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">langchain#41766</a>)</li>
+<li><strong>TypeSafe 0.1</strong>: <code>TypeSafeClassifier</code> implements the Decisions API and adds image inputs and <code>Box</code> answers. Its experimental middleware imports are deprecated: use <code>langchain.agents.middleware</code> with <code>classifier=TypeSafeClassifier()</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/integrations/providers/typesafe" class="link">TypeSafe integration guide</a> for usage and migration details.</li>
+<li><strong>Consistent refusal handling</strong>: OpenAI and Anthropic refusals use standard <code>refusal</code> content blocks. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/models#handle-a-model-refusal" class="link">Handle a model refusal</a>.</li>
+<li><strong>Summarization improvements</strong>: <code>SummarizationMiddleware</code> accepts a separate model or custom callable through <code>summarizer=</code>.</li>
+<li><strong>Mid-conversation updates</strong>: Supported models accept <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/anthropic#mid-conversation-system-messages" class="link">Anthropic system and tool updates</a>, <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#add-tools-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI Responses tool additions</a>, and <a href="/oss/python/integrations/chat/openai#changing-reasoning-effort-mid-conversation" class="link">OpenAI reasoning-effort changes</a> without restarting the conversation.</li>
+</ul></div></div></div>

For example, earlier versions of <code>@langchain/react</code> can stop updating a <code>useMessages</code> view.  <li>Upgrade every client that reads the stream:
 <ul>
 <li>In JavaScript, upgrade <code>@langchain/react</code>, <code>@langchain/vue</code>, <code>@langchain/svelte</code>, or <code>@langchain/angular</code> to 1.2.6 or later.</li>
@@ -1125,34 +1134,34 @@
 </ul>
 </li>

-</ol><span data-as="p"><code>langchain</code> and <code>deepagents</code> accept any <code>langchain-core</code> 1.x, so upgrading either one can pull in 1.7.0. To hold the server back until you upgrade every client, pin <code>langchain-core&lt;1.7.0</code>.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes" aria-labelledby="_R_1s35crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s35crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><span data-as="p">Starting in <code>langchain-core</code> 1.6.13, iterating <code>message.tool_calls</code> yields only the new part of each tool call’s arguments, with the call’s <code>id</code> and <code>name</code>. Earlier versions yielded all of the arguments so far. A call’s chunks concatenate into its full arguments, and <code>message.tool_calls.get()</code> still returns the parsed tool calls.</span></div></div></div>

+</ol><span data-as="p"><code>langchain</code> and <code>deepagents</code> accept any <code>langchain-core</code> 1.x, so upgrading either one can pull in 1.7.0. To hold the server back until you upgrade every client, pin <code>langchain-core&lt;1.7.0</code>.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes" aria-labelledby="_R_1s55crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s55crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><span data-as="p">Starting in <code>langchain-core</code> 1.6.13, iterating <code>message.tool_calls</code> yields only the new part of each tool call’s arguments, with the call’s <code>id</code> and <code>name</code>. Earlier versions yielded all of the arguments so far. A call’s chunks concatenate into its full arguments, and <code>message.tool_calls.get()</code> still returns the parsed tool calls.</span></div></div></div>

 <li><strong>Typed answers</strong>: each interrupt carries a <code>response_schema</code> listing the decisions that call allows, so a client can build its form from the schema. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#read-the-response-schema" class="link">Read the response schema</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Checked before saving</strong>: LangGraph rejects an invalid answer before it is saved, so you can correct it and answer the same interrupt again. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#correct-an-invalid-answer" class="link">Correct an invalid answer</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Edits stay on the called tool</strong>: an <code>edit</code> can’t switch tools, and its arguments are checked against the tool’s own schema.</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Use per-call mode for new agents. LangChain plans to make it the default in the next major release. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/human-in-the-loop#pause-once-per-tool-call" class="link">Pause once per tool call</a> and the <a href="/oss/python/concepts/human-in-the-loop" class="link">human-in-the-loop overview</a>.</span></div></div></div>

[&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">The <code>langchain.mcp</code> namespace is in beta. Importing from it raises a <code>LangChainBetaWarning</code>.</span></div></div><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>One adapter, any transport</strong>: <code>MCPAdapter</code> infers the transport from its target — a URL, a local script over stdio, an in-process server, an <code>MCPConfig</code> dict for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/connections#multiple-servers" class="link">multiple servers</a>, or a pre-built <code>fastmcp.Client</code>. <code>await adapter.list_tools()</code> returns LangChain tools ready for <code>create_agent</code>. See the <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp" class="link">MCP overview</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Interrupt-driven elicitation</strong>: when a server asks for input mid-call, <code>MCPAdapter</code> surfaces the question as a LangGraph <code>interrupt()</code>, so a human answers and the run resumes. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#elicitation" class="link">Elicitation</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Authentication through FastMCP</strong>: bearer tokens, full OAuth 2.1 with dynamic client registration, or any <code>httpx.Auth</code>, including <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth#per-user-authentication" class="link">per-user authentication</a> in a deployment. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/auth" class="link">Authentication</a>.</li>
 <li><strong>Richer tool metadata</strong>: each tool carries its MCP provenance under an <code>mcp</code> namespace on its metadata, including annotations such as <code>destructive_hint</code> for <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#human-in-the-loop" class="link">gating destructive tools behind approval</a>. See <a href="/oss/python/langchain/mcp/tools#tool-metadata" class="link">Tool metadata</a>.</li>
-</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="migrating" aria-labelledby="_R_1s75crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#migrating" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Migrating">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s75crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Migrating</span></h3><span data-as="p">Moving from <code>langchain-mcp-adapters</code>? <code>MultiServerMCPClient</code> is replaced by <code>MCPAdapter</code>, and several features have changed or been removed. whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="deepagents-v0-7-0" aria-labelledby="_R_cb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#deepagents-v0-7-0" class="-ml-10 flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer"><code>deepagents</code> v0.7.0</span></h2><span data-as="p">A leaner, more configurable harness by default. On a default-agent turn, input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895), validated against our <a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/how-we-benchmark-deep-agents" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">revamped evaluation suite</a> with no quality regression.</span><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="optimizations" aria-labelledby="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#optimizations" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Optimizations">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_sb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Optimizations</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Lean prompts by default</strong>: The authored base prompt starts empty and tool-usage prose that duplicated tool schemas has been trimmed. Isolated to the default agent’s tool schemas, total description tokens drop <strong>43%</strong> (4,005 → 2,302); combined with the empty base prompt and opt-in todos, a default-agent turn’s input tokens drop <strong>65%</strong> (5,395 → 1,895). Tool behavior is unchanged. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4859" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4859</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4979" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4979</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/5009" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#5009</a>)</li>
-</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1c95crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
+</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="features-3" aria-labelledby="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#features-3" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Features">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1cb5crmhkd74t9_" class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Features</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/customization#middleware" class="link">Override a default middleware instance</a></strong>: A <code>middleware=</code> (or subagent <code>middleware</code>) instance whose <code>.name</code> matches a built-in now replaces that default in place, rather than erroring on a duplicate. For example, pass your own <code>SummarizationMiddleware(...)</code> to change the token trigger or summary model without disabling the built-in default. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4251" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4251</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Filesystem tools</strong>: New <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/tools#built-in-harness-tools" class="link"><code>delete</code></a> tool removes a file or recursively removes a directory (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3659" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#3659</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/3851" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#3851</a>); <code>write_file</code> now overwrites an existing file instead of erroring (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4109" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4109</a>); <code>FilesystemMiddleware</code> accepts a <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/overview#virtual-filesystem-access" class="link">tool allowlist</a> to expose only selected built-in tools (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4325" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4325</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4698" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4698</a>); and reads and searches are tuned for open models — paginated <code>read_file</code> reports total and remaining lines plus the next <code>offset</code> (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4540" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4540</a>), <code>grep</code>/<code>glob</code> return partial results with a <code>truncated</code> flag instead of hanging on large trees (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4063" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4063</a>), and <code>grep</code> gains a 1,000-match cap with streamed output and optional context lines (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4570" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4570</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4706" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4706</a>).</li>
 <li><strong>More prompt-caching support</strong>: Bedrock prompt caching via the <code>deepagents[aws]</code> extra (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/issues/4108" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4108</a>), and automatic Fireworks prompt-cache session affinity (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4598" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4598</a>).</li>
 <li><strong>NVIDIA support</strong>: A built-in Nemotron 3 Ultra harness profile plus NIM app-origin attribution. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4192" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4192</a>, <a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4455" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4455</a>)</li>
-</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1s95crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><ul>
+</ul><h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="breaking-changes-2" aria-labelledby="_R_1sb5crmhkd74t9_"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#breaking-changes-2" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Breaking changes">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-3 shrink-0"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Link"></use></svg></div></a></div><span id="_R_1sb5crmhkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Breaking changes</span></h3><ul>
 <li><strong>Planning todos are opt-in</strong>: <code>create_deep_agent</code> no longer includes <code>TodoListMiddleware</code> by default, so the <code>write_todos</code> tool, <code>todos</code> state channel, and todo-planning prompt are absent unless restored with <code>middleware=[TodoListMiddleware()]</code>. (The OpenAI Codex harness profile still opts in automatically.) (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4929" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4929</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Backend compatibility shims removed</strong>: Pass concrete <code>BackendProtocol</code> instances instead of factories, configure <code>StoreBackend</code> with an explicit <code>namespace</code>, and use the current <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> / <code>grep</code> / <code>ReadResult</code> APIs. Removed symbols include <code>BackendFactory</code>, <code>BACKEND_TYPES</code>, <code>FileFormat</code>, and <code>Unset</code>. New files store string <code>FileData.content</code>; older <code>list[str]</code> content stays readable and converts on next write. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4541" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4541</a>)</li>
 <li><strong>Output format changes</strong>: Empty <code>ls</code> / <code>glob</code> output is now <code>No files found</code> instead of <code>[]</code>, and <code>read_file</code> no longer renders a fixed-width <code>cat -n</code>-style gutter — update any parsers of raw tool output. (<a href="https://github.com/langchain-ai/deepagents/pull/4561" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">#4561</a>)</li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Copy the following prompt into your AI coding assistant to migrate a codebase for these breaking changes:</span><div class="prompt my-6 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 border-stone-100 bg-stone-50 dark:border-stone-800 dark:bg-stone-900 text-sm/[20px] text-stone-900 tracking-[-0.1px] dark:text-stone-50 print:print-color-exact"><div class="not-prose flex min-w-0 items-start gap-2" data-component-part="prompt-content-wrapper"><span aria-hidden="true" class="flex h-[1lh] shrink-0 select-none items-center justify-center" data-component-part="prompt-icon"><svg class="forced-colors:forced-color-adjust-none forced-colors:bg-[color:CanvasText]! bg-gray-800 dark:bg-gray-100 m-0! size-3.5 shrink-0" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" style="-webkit-mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/tabler/v3.44.0/outline/arrow-right.svg&quot;);-webkit-mask-repeat:no-repeat;-webkit-mask-position:center;mask-image:url(&quot;https://d3gk2c5xim1je2.cloudfront.net/tabler/v3.44.0/outline/arrow-right.svg&quot;);mask-repeat:no-repeat;mask-position:center;mask-size:100%"></svg></span><div class="min-w-0" data-component-part="prompt-description"><div class="space-y-4 whitespace-normal"><p>Migrate a deepagents codebase from v0.6.x to v0.7.</p></div></div></div><div class="ml-auto flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 print:hidden" data-component-part="prompt-actions-wrapper"><button class="px-2 py-1 flex items-center gap-1 justify-center rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-colors duration-70 outline-offset-4 text-xs/[16px] whitespace-nowrap font-medium border-transparent bg-primary text-white hover:bg-primary-dark" data-component-part="prompt-action-copy-button" type="button" aria-label="Copy prompt"><span aria-hidden="true" class="grid [&amp;&gt;*]:col-start-1 [&amp;&gt;*]:row-start-1 [&amp;&gt;*]:flex [&amp;&gt;*]:items-center [&amp;&gt;*]:justify-center [&amp;&gt;*]:gap-1"><span class="invisible"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="2" class="size-2.5 shrink-0 text-white dark:text-white"><use href="#mi-mint-1-Check"></use></svg>Copied</span><span class="">Copy prompt</span></span><span role="status" class="sr-only"></span></button></div></div></div></div></div>

 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/interpreters" class="link"><code>CodeInterpreterMiddleware</code></a></strong>: (experimental) <code>deepagents</code> now supports code execution and programmatic tool calling through a scoped QuickJS runtime.</li>
 <li>Supports <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> in <code>stream_events</code> / <code>astream_events</code>. Refer to the <a href="/oss/python/deepagents/event-streaming" class="link">event streaming</a> guide for details.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/delta-channels-evolving-agent-runtime" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): Deep Agents now uses <code>DeltaChannel</code> for message history and agent files. Rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value into every checkpoint, only the incremental delta written at each step is stored — keeping checkpoint sizes small as threads grow long.</li>
@@ -1160,8 +1169,8 @@
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/profiles" class="link">Harness profiles</a></strong>: Register per-provider or per-model configuration bundles (<code>HarnessProfile</code>) that <code>create_deep_agent</code> applies automatically when a model is selected — system-prompt tweaks, tool overrides, middleware changes, and subagent defaults — without modifying the call site.</li>
 <li><strong><a href="/oss/python/deepagents/backends#contexthubbackend" class="link"><code>ContextHubBackend</code></a></strong> (<a href="https://www.langchain.com/blog/introducing-context-hub" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">blog</a>): A new filesystem backend backed by LangSmith Hub. Agent files — skills, memories, and other persisted context — are stored as Hub commits, giving you version history on every write and LangSmith-native durability without provisioning a separate LangGraph store.</li>
 </ul></div></div></div>

data-as="p">This release adds finer-grained control over node execution (timeouts, error recovery, and graceful shutdown), a new channel type that cuts checkpoint overhead for long-running threads, and a new content-block-centric streaming API (v3) with typed, per-channel projections.</span><ul>
 <li>
 <span data-as="p"><strong><a href="/oss/python/langgraph/pregel#deltachannel" class="link"><code>DeltaChannel</code></a> (beta)</strong>: A new channel type that stores only the incremental delta at each step rather than re-serializing the full accumulated value. Most useful for channels that grow large over time, for example a message list in a long-running thread. Use <code>snapshot_frequency=K</code> to write a full snapshot every K steps and bound read latency.</span>
 </li>
@@ -1178,7 +1187,7 @@
 <span data-as="p"><strong>New event streaming API (beta)</strong>: Pass <code>version=&quot;v3&quot;</code> to <code>stream_events()</code> / <code>astream_events()</code> for a content-block-centric protocol with typed, per-channel projections (<code>run.values</code>, <code>run.messages</code>, <code>run.lifecycle</code>, <code>run.subgraphs</code>) plus opt-in transformers for updates, custom events, checkpoints, tasks, and debug. <code>run.messages</code> yields one <code>ChatModelStream</code> per LLM call with typed sub-projections for text, reasoning, tool calls, and usage. <code>version=&quot;v1&quot;</code> and <code>version=&quot;v2&quot;</code> are unchanged.</span>
 </li>
 </ul><span data-as="p">Timeouts and error handlers are Python-only; retry policies continue to work in both Python and TypeScript.</span></div></div></div>

-- [ ] [Publish @types/vscode](https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/wiki/Publish-vscode-types) if you find type diffs after running the steps

-  - [ ] Push the tag: `git push origin <x.y.z>`

-- [ ] Close milestone (**do not** delete it): https://github.com/microsoft/vscode/milestones
-- [ ] Move all remaining issues to "On Deck"
